{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EinEDkYCb8J3LvQmNYpyJh","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# AGENTS.md\n     2→\n     3→Guidance for AI coding agents working in this repository.\n     4→\n     5→## What this is\n     6→\n     7→A source-linked public graph of Washington State legislative activity — bills, hearings, testimony, video, transcripts, reviewed speakers, organizations, and public-record context — modeled on CalMatters Digital Democracy. Long-form ingestion walkthrough lives at **`docs/ingestion.md`** — read that before making changes to anything in `internal/jobs/` or `internal/sources/`.\n     8→\n     9→## Architecture in one minute\n    10→\n    11→```\n    12→nightly cron: make daily\n    13→  ├─ wa-dd ingest-legislators  LWS roster → person, legislator, memberships\n    14→  ├─ wa-dd ingest-session     LWS metadata for every bill in biennium +\n    15→  │                           CSI agenda IDs + TVW event IDs on hearing rows\n    16→  ├─ wa-dd ingest-irs-bmf-wa  IRS BMF Washington nonprofit reference data\n    17→  ├─ wa-dd ingest-pdc-employers\n    18→  │                           PDC lobbyist-employer registrations\n    19→  ├─ wa-dd ingest-hearings    full hearing pipeline (CSI testifiers +\n    20→  │                           Invintus event/media + diarization +\n    21→  │                           transcript segmentation + organization links)\n    22→  ├─ wa-dd verify-organizations\n    23→  │                           confirms existing orgs against IRS/PDC refs\n    24→  └─ wa-dd generate-vendor-entity-matches\n    25→                              creates reviewable source/org match candidates\n    26→\n    27→  → Postgres (single source of truth)\n    28→  → wa-dd-api on :8080 (chi router; reads only; Clerk-gated /admin routes)\n    29→  → Next.js on :3000 (Server Components fetch the API; ISR revalidate: 60;\n    30→                      /admin UI for speaker + org review)\n    31→```\n    32→\n    33→The `bill` / `legislator` / `bill_sponsor` / `bill_status_change` / `hearing` / `agenda_item` / `tvw_event` / `organization` tables are upsertable on stable keys — re-running passes is safe. `testifier` and `agenda_item_window` are replaced per agenda item; diarization rows are appended per provider job, but `ingest-hearings` checks for an existing succeeded diarization job and uses an event-level advisory lock to avoid duplicate provider submissions.\n    34→\n    35→## Layout\n    36→\n    37→```\n    38→cmd/\n    39→  wa-dd/        operator CLI. One file per subcommand (cmd_*.go). Covers\n    40→                ingestion (ingest-session, ingest-hearings,\n    41→                ingest-legislators, ingest-contracts, ingest-pdc-*,\n    42→                ingest-irs-bmf-wa, ingest-usaspending-wa-awards, …),\n    43→                diarization (audio-cache, diarize-event, diarize-pending,\n    44→                extract-speaker-evidence,\n    45→                backfill-speaker-evidence), and entity/org workflows\n    46→                (populate-organizations,\n    47→                generate-vendor-entity-matches, decide-entity-match,\n    48→                verify-organizations, prune-junk-organizations).\n    49→  wa-dd-api/    read-only HTTP API the Next.js frontend reads from (:8080).\n    50→                Routes split per resource (bill/hearing/org/...); registry in\n    51→                routes.go.\n    52→internal/\n    53→  sources/{lws,csi,committeeschedules,tvw,pdc,socrata,datawa,fiscalwa,\n    54→           usaspending,irsbmf,bls,census,epa,fema,hud,sao,seattle,\n    55→           seattleauditor,kingcounty,webs,connector,...}/\n    56→                connectors. Each follows Fetch / StoreRaw / Parse / Normalize.\n    57→                Phase status table lives in internal/sources/README.md.\n    58→  sources/httpx/ shared retry + per-host rate-limit client\n    59→  jobs/         pipeline step primitives (ingest-bill, ingest-csi, ingest-tvw,\n    60→                segment-transcript, populate-organizations, discover)\n    61→  diarization/  provider-neutral diarization (Provider interface, Deepgram +\n    62→                pyannoteAI implementations, MergeConsecutiveSegments,\n    63→                self-introduction evidence extractor).\n    64→  entitymatch/  organization/vendor entity-match candidate generation +\n    65→                decision recording.\n    66→  common/       shared civic value objects (BillKey, BillAgendaTarget)\n    67→  storage/db/   pgx wrapper, hand-written Pool.Query methods on *Store,\n    68→                BillAgendaTarget lookups and query helpers\n    69→db/migrations/  goose-style SQL; project-pinned via tools/goose\n    70→db/queries/     EMPTY. sqlc.yaml exists but the project uses hand-written\n    71→                Pool.Query methods on *Store, not codegen. Don't add to this\n    72→                directory unless explicitly migrating to sqlc.\n    73→apps/web/       Next.js 16 + React 19 + TS + Tailwind 4. Server Components\n    74→                fetch the Go API by absolute URL (process.env.WADD_API_URL)\n    75→                because they don't go through next.config.ts rewrites.\n    76→                Client components use the rewrite (/api/v1/* → :8080).\n    77→                /admin uses Clerk in production; bypassed in non-prod via\n    78→                NODE_ENV gate (see src/lib/adminAuth.ts and src/proxy.ts).\n    79→data/processed/ run-summary JSONs, diarization output, and derived\n    80→                artifacts (gitignored)\n    81→docs/           ingestion.md is the canonical implementation doc.\n    82→                hearing-diarization-and-review.md covers the diarization +\n    83→                speaker-review pipeline. organization-matching-and-review.md\n    84→                covers the org/vendor entity-match flow.\n    85→```\n    86→\n    87→## Common commands\n    88→\n    89→```sh\n    90→# Local dev environment\n    91→make up                     # start Postgres in Docker\n    92→make migrate-up             # apply migrations via project-pinned goose\n    93→make api                    # run wa-dd-api on :8080\n    94→cd apps/web && pnpm dev     # Next.js on :3000\n    95→\n    96→# Tests + gates — see \"Running tests\" below for full setup per category\n    97→go vet ./...\n    98→go test ./...                                       # Go unit tests, no infra\n    99→make integration                                    # Go integration tests (boots Postgres, seeds/cleans fixtures)\n   100→cd apps/web && pnpm typecheck                       # Frontend typecheck\n   101→make e2e                                            # Playwright e2e (seeds/cleans fixtures; requires Postgres + chromium)\n   102→\n   103→# Ingestion (operator-driven; usually triggered via make daily)\n   104→INVINTUS_EMBEDDER_KEY=… PYANNOTEAI_API_KEY=… make daily  # full nightly chain\n   105→go run ./cmd/wa-dd ingest-session --biennium 2025-26 --limit 25  # smoke\n   106→go run ./cmd/wa-dd ingest-hearings  --biennium 2025-26 --limit 5\n   107→\n   108→```\n   109→\n   110→`make help` lists every Makefile target with a one-line description.\n   111→\n   112→## Running tests\n   113→\n   114→Four categories. **Always use these exact commands** — don't improvise (e.g. `pnpm exec playwright` directly will fail without env vars).\n   115→\n   116→### 1. Go unit tests (no infra)\n   117→\n   118→```sh\n   119→go test ./...\n   120→go test ./internal/sources/lws/... -run TestParse   # single package + test\n   121→go vet ./...\n   122→```\n   123→\n   124→### 2. Go integration tests (need Postgres)\n   125→\n   126→```sh\n   127→make integration              # boots integration DB, seeds fixtures, runs go test -tags=integration ./..., cleans fixtures\n   128→# under the hood:\n   129→#   make integration-db       # docker compose up + migrate\n   130→#   scripts/seed-test-fixtures.sh\n   131→#   WADD_TEST_DSN=… go test -tags=integration ./...\n   132→#   scripts/cleanup-test-fixtures.sh\n   133→```\n   134→\n   135→For a single integration package: `WADD_TEST_DSN=\"postgres://wadd:wadd@localhost:5432/wa_dd?sslmode=disable\" go test -tags=integration ./cmd/wa-dd-api/...` (DB must already be up + seeded). If you seed fixtures manually, run `scripts/cleanup-test-fixtures.sh --dsn \"$WADD_TEST_DSN\"` afterward.\n   136→\n   137→### 3. Frontend typecheck / lint\n   138→\n   139→```sh\n   140→cd apps/web && pnpm install      # first time only\n   141→cd apps/web && pnpm typecheck\n   142→cd apps/web && pnpm lint\n   143→```\n   144→\n   145→### 4. End-to-end (Playwright + axe a11y)\n   146→\n   147→E2E spins up `wa-dd-api` and `next start` against a real Postgres, seeds deterministic fixtures for the run, cleans them afterward, then drives Chromium. **First-time setup is required** or every test will fail with \"browser not installed\" / \"module not found\".\n   148→\n   149→```sh\n   150→# One-time setup\n   151→make up                                 # Postgres in Docker\n   152→make migrate-up\n   153→cd apps/web && pnpm install             # installs @playwright/test, @axe-core/playwright, etc.\n   154→make e2e-install                        # downloads Chromium for Playwright\n   155→\n   156→# Run\n   157→make e2e                                # seeds fixtures, builds wa-dd-api + Next.js,\n   158→                                        # runs Playwright, then cleans fixtures\n   159→\n   160→# Single test (DB already seeded, binary already built)\n   161→cd apps/web && WADD_API_BIN=\"$(pwd)/../../bin/wa-dd-api\" \\\n   162→  WADD_E2E_DSN=\"postgres://wadd:wadd@localhost:5432/wa_dd?sslmode=disable\" \\\n   163→  pnpm exec playwright test -g \"accessibility landmarks\" --reporter=list\n   164→\n   165→# After manually seeded single-test runs\n   166→scripts/cleanup-test-fixtures.sh --dsn \"postgres://wadd:wadd@localhost:5432/wa_dd?sslmode=disable\"\n   167→```\n   168→\n   169→E2E tests live in `apps/web/e2e/`. Accessibility checks use `@axe-core/playwright` against WCAG 2.0/2.1 A+AA — a real violation fails the build, so fix the markup/styles rather than suppressing rules.\n   170→\n   171→## Conventions worth knowing\n   172→\n   173→- **Time zones.** WA legislative timestamps (LWS, TVW WP archive) are Pacific wall-clock without an explicit zone. Always parse with `time.ParseInLocation(..., \"America/Los_Angeles\")`. There's a fixed bug history here — see `internal/sources/lws/normalize.go:parseLWSDate` and `internal/jobs/discover.go:tvwPostsForDay`.\n   174→- **Bill prefixes.** LWS reports `BillID` in the *current* substituted/engrossed form (`SSB 6054`, `2SHB 1859`). `internal/sources/lws/normalize.go:baseBillPrefix` strips `E`/`N`/`S` chrome down to the bare prefix (`HB`/`SB`/`HJR`/etc.) so a single bill doesn't fork into multiple rows as it moves through the legislature.\n   175→- **`IngestBill` two-mode behavior.** When `Demo.Chamber` is set (curated path), it stores one chamber-matched hearing. When empty (`ingest-session` path), it stores every hearing LWS reports so the `ingest-session` discovery post-pass has rows to enrich.\n   176→- **Rate limit default 10 req/sec** per upstream host. The User-Agent identifies the project so state-agency operators can contact us. Retries on 429/5xx with exponential backoff.\n   177→- **Pipeline orchestration** lives in the CLI drivers. `Pipeline.RunMetadataOnly` runs only `IngestBill` for `ingest-session`; `ingest-hearings` iterates by `hearing_id`, runs CSI per agenda item, Invintus + diarization once per TVW event, transcript segmentation per agenda item, then hearing-scoped organization population.\n   178→- **Organizations.** CSI `raw_organization` strings seed `organization` rows as `possible`; `/organizations` shows only confirmed organizations. `verify-organizations` confirms unique IRS BMF/PDC employer normalized-name matches (IRS preferred over PDC). Confirmed entity-match decisions append the reviewed source name to `organization.aliases`, and organization upserts merge aliases instead of replacing them.\n   179→- **API response shapes.** Public API routes should return explicit response/list objects (`BillDetailResponse`, `HearingPage`, `OrganizationPage`, etc.). Keep collection fields initialized to `[]` rather than `nil` so frontend code can treat them as arrays.\n   180→- **No generated page snapshots.** Public/frontend page data comes from route-specific API objects assembled from Postgres.\n   181→- **API handler pattern.** `func handler(store *db.Store) http.HandlerFunc` returning a closure. Use the `writeJSON` envelope and `{\"error\": \"...\"}` for errors. Soft-parse query params (bad `limit=abc` falls back to default rather than 400) — see `billPageHandler` and `searchTranscriptsHandler` for examples.\n   182→- **Frontend fetch path.** Server Components fetch by absolute URL (`process.env.WADD_API_URL ?? \"http://localhost:8080\"`) because they don't traverse `next.config.ts` rewrites. Client components use the rewrite path `/api/v1/...` so requests stay same-origin.\n   183→- **Diarization providers.** `internal/diarization` is provider-neutral. pyannoteAI (`precision-2`) is the default for hearing ingestion and can return bundled transcription; Deepgram (`nova-3`) remains available as an alternate provider. See `docs/hearing-diarization-and-review.md` for tradeoffs and run commands.\n   184→- **Local admin auth.** `/admin` routes bypass Clerk when `NODE_ENV !== \"production\"` — see `apps/web/src/lib/adminAuth.ts`, `apps/web/src/proxy.ts`, and `cmd/wa-dd-api/auth.go`'s `localDevAdminBypass`. The web Dockerfile has a separate `web-dev` stage (`NODE_ENV=development`, `next dev`) used by `infra/docker-compose.yml`; the final `web` stage stays production-default for Railway.\n   185→- **Backwards compatibility** Typically, you do not need to make changes backwards compatible. Only include backwards compatibility if the user explicitly says so.\n   186→\n   187→## What lives where in the wiki\n   188→\n   189→- `~/Documents/v1/wiki/politics/Washington Digital Democracy - Comprehensive Plan.md` — current product/architecture record. The MVP phases through public beta are marked complete; do not reintroduce stale \"next steps\" or Phase 4 roadmap language unless Nolan asks.\n   190→- `~/Documents/v1/wiki/politics/Washington Digital Democracy - Canonical Data Sources.md` — source-of-truth list of upstream data sources.\n   191→- `~/Documents/v1/wiki/politics/data-sources/` — per-source connector specs.\n   192→- The wiki is the project-level intent; `docs/ingestion.md` is the implementation walkthrough.\n   193→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it looks malicious. If it does, you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer high-level questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bd2d97ff-60b4-432b-889b-05065dbb42cc","uuid":"1e93e9bc-aa6c-429a-9891-ba82b541c1b8"}

 poa.source_kind::text,\n   303→       CASE poa.source_kind::text\n   304→         WHEN 'csi_testifier' THEN 'Committee Sign In'\n   305→         WHEN 'pdc_lobbyist_employment' THEN 'PDC lobbyist array_to_string(\n   316→           array_agg(DISTINCT poa.record_year ORDER BY poa.record_year DESC)\n   317→             FILTER (WHERE poa.record_year IS NOT NULL),\n   318→           ', '\n   319→ 328→       END AS confidence\n   329→  FROM person_organization_affiliation poa\n   330→  LEFT JOIN person p ON p.id = poa.person_id\n   331→ WHERE poa.organization_id = $1\n   332→ GROUP BY p.id,        WHEN 'lobbyist_for' THEN 0\n   336→            WHEN 'testified_for' THEN 1\n   337→            WHEN 'signed_in_for' THEN 2\n   338→            ELSE 3\n   339→          END,\n   340→ 359→func (s *Store) GetOrganizationPublicContexts(ctx context.Context, organizationID int64) ([]OrganizationPublicContext, error) {\n   360→\tconst q = `\n   361→WITH ctx AS (\n   362→  SELECT
